企業簡介

China Lesso Group Holdings Limited (Stock Code of Hong Kong Stock          中國聯塑集團控股有限公司（香港聯交所股份代
Exchange: 2128) is a leading large-scale industrial group that             號：2128）是中國領先的大型管道建材產業集團。

years of rapid development, the Group has evolved into a leader in the     材家居、環保、新能源、供應鏈服務平台等領域
industry of building materials and home improvement. It provides high-     為人們提供優質的產品和服務，快速成長為建材
quality products and services such as plastic piping, building materials   家居行業的佼佼者、行業的標杆。
and home improvement, new energy, environmental protection, and
operates a supply chain service platform.

bases in 19 provinces of China and in foreign countries. The Group has     地，分佈於中國 19 個省份及海外國家。本集團擁
established a nationwide sales network and has also developed long-        有覆 蓋全 國的廣 泛分銷 網絡，與 2,853 名 的獨立
term strategic partnerships with 2,853 independent and exclusive first-    獨家一級經銷商建立長期戰略合作關係，能夠及
tier distributors that enable timely and efficient supply of               時及高效地為客戶提供優質豐富的產品和專業的
comprehensive, quality products and professional services to               服務。作為中國管道建材領域產品體系最為齊全

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告書

2023 was the year when China just began to recover from the                 2023 年是疫後復蘇的一年，國家經濟工作堅持「穩
pandemic outbreak. The country adhered to the tone of its economic          中求進」總基調，推動高質量發展，全力做好「穩
work — “pursuing progress amid stability” by promoting high-quality         增 長、穩 就 業、防 風 險」工 作。然 而，國 家 面 對
development while working at full steam to stabilise growth and             全球宏觀經濟和地緣政治波動的大環境影響，企
employment rate and mitigate risks. Nevertheless, businesses were still     業的經營環境仍然充滿挑戰和不明朗性。
facing challenges and uncertainties in an operating environment
marked by the global economic volatility and geopolitical turbulence.

As China’s leading piping and building materials conglomerate, China        中國聯塑多年來本著自生的競爭優勢，作為中國
Lesso has developed over the years into a manufacturer with a               領先的大型管道建材產業集團，並擁有過萬多種
comprehensive product system of building materials and home                 產品，是中國建材家居領域產品體系齊全的生產
improvement in the country, boasting its own competitive advantages         商。本集團將繼續把握利好的國家政策對基礎設
and offering over 10,000 types of products. The Group will continue to      施和管道的穩定需求，順勢而為，在波動的大環
capitalise on the stable demand for infrastructure and piping arising       境中穩中求進，為股東和投資者繼續創造理想回
from the favourable government policies as it seeks to make steady          報。2023 年，本 集 團 總 收 入 為 人 民 幣 308.68 億
progress and generate good returns for the shareholders and investors       元，本公司擁有人應佔溢利為人民幣 23.68 億元。
in the volatile business environment. For the year 2023, the Group          董 事 會 建 議 宣 派 截 至 2023 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度 之
recorded a total revenue of RMB30,868 million and a profit attributable     末期股息為每股 20 港仙。
to owners of the Company of RMB2,368 million. The Board proposed
payment of a final dividend of HK20 cents per share for the year ended
31 December 2023.

For 37 years, China Lesso has unwaveringly adhered to its development       37 年 來，中 國 聯 塑 堅 定「深 耕 管 業、乘 勢 綠 能、
direction, namely “further developing the piping business and               互惠共贏」的發展思路，並「以質量為生命，以科
capitalising on the emergence of green energy for mutual benefit”, and      技為龍頭，以顧客滿意為宗旨」為經營方針，致
has also been striving for customer satisfaction by prioritising quality    力為社會創造高價值的產品與服務。目前，本集
and driving its development with industry-leading technology, thus          團的業務涵蓋管道、建材家居、環保、新能源、
creating high-value products and services for society. Currently, the       供應鏈服務平台等板塊。隨著國際化、全球化進
Group has an extensive scope of businesses that encompass piping,           程 步 伐 的 推 進，中 國 聯 塑 已 建 立 超 過 30 個 先 進
building materials and home improvement, environmental protection,          的生產基地，分佈於全國 19 個省份及海外國家，
new energy, and a supply chain service platform. Keeping pace with          並積極完善戰略佈局，拓寬銷售網絡和市場。
internationalisation and globalisation, China Lesso has established over
30 advanced production bases in 19 provinces of China and in overseas
countries. It has also been proactively optimising its business portfolio
and market coverage according to its business strategy and
broadening both its sales network and market.

Piping business has always been China Lesso’s core and mainstay             一直以來，管道是中國聯塑的核心主營業務和核
business and the primary source of profit. The Group has consistently       心盈利來源。本集團一直保持管道系統業務的龍
maintained its leading position in the piping systems market and will be    頭地位，通過推進智能製造、產品創新、生產升
able to continue leading the development of the piping industry by          級、提質增效，以高標準、高質量、可持續引領
expediting intelligent manufacturing, product innovation, the upgrading     管道行業發展，同時有助提升本集團的整體運營
of production, the improvement of both quality and efficiency, and by       效率、擴大市場份額和提升盈利能力。
maintaining high quality standards. The move will also help the Group to
enhance its operational efficiency, expand its market share and bolster
its profitability.

To create better urban and living spaces, the Group actively offers              為打造美好城市與生活空間，本集團積極向社會

green products in its businesses of piping and environmental                     產品，主動管理營運對於環境的影響，減少營運
protection. Meanwhile, it will proactively manage the environmental              環境足跡，構建多元和諧的工作和合作環境，追
impacts of its business operations by reducing their environmental               求全面可持續發展，為力爭國家「雙碳」戰略而努
footprint. It will also create a diverse and harmonious environment for          力。
work and cooperation while pursuing sustainable development and
striving to attain the dual national goals of carbon emissions peak and
carbon neutrality.

In the future, China Lesso will continue to fully capitalise on the ongoing      展望未來，中國聯塑將繼續充分把握國家政策及
demand for infrastructure construction on the back of the state policy           經濟恢復向好對基礎設施建設的持續需求，積極
and economic recovery by proactively developing and expanding its                深耕管道領域與擴大產業佈局，做強做精主產業
piping business, enhancing the products at this mainstay business,               產品，優化產品組合和提升擴大市場佔有率。同
optimising the product portfolio and expanding its market share.                 時，本集 團通 過 AI、互聯網、物 聯網等技術，結
Meanwhile, the Group has established smart factories that integrate              合數字化智慧運營管理，打造智能製造和自動化
intelligent manufacturing and automated production by adopting                   生產為一體的智能工廠，提高企業核心實力。
technologies such as artificial intelligence, the Internet and the IoT in
combination with digitalised and intelligentized operation and
management with an aim to enhancing its core competency.

While continuing to face myriad challenges and uncertainties in the              雖然未來仍然面對重重挑戰和不確定性，但本人
future, I am confident about leading the management team as a                    有信心將繼續帶領管理團隊上下一心，順應市場
cohesive group, adapting to market trends through the upgrading of               趨勢而不斷升級創新，把握海外機遇，提升優化
business operation and innovation, grasping opportunities in overseas            管理水平，穩中求進，為社會、客戶和消費者提
markets, raising the standards of management, making steady                      供優質產品和服務，把握產業的高質量發展。
progress with prudence, providing quality products and services for
society, clients and consumers, and capitalising on the industry’s high-
quality development.

The Board and I would like to express our heartfelt gratitude to all our         本人連同董事會所有成員，向全體員工付出的貢
staff for their contributions. China Lesso will strive for good results, share   獻致以衷心謝意。中國聯塑將致力爭取更理想的
the fruits with its customers, business partners and employees, and              成績，實現客戶、合作夥伴、員工分享共贏成果，
continue to create value for society and the shareholders.                       為社會和股東創造價值。

BIOGRAPHICAL DETAILS OF DIRECTORS AND
SENIOR MANAGEMENT
董事及高級管理人員履歷

EXECUTIVE DIRECTORS                                                           執行董事
Mr. Wong Luen Hei, aged 61, is the founder of the Group, the chairman of      黃聯禧先生，61 歲，為本集團創辦人兼本公司主
the Company and was appointed as an executive director of the                 席，於 2009 年 11 月 5 日 獲 委 任 為 本 公 司 執 行 董
Company on 5 November 2009. He is also a director of various                  事。彼亦於本集團內多間成員公司擔任董事職務。
companies within the Group. He is primarily responsible for the Group’s       彼主要負責本集團的整體戰略策劃及業務管理。
overall strategic planning and business management. Mr. Wong has              黃 先 生 於 塑 料 管 道 經 營 及 管 理 方 面 累 積 約 27 年
approximately 27 years of experience in plastic pipe operations and           經驗。彼於 1996 年 12 月至 1999 年 4 月擔任佛山市
management. He served as the chairman in Foshan Shunde Liansu                 順 德 區 聯 塑 實 業 有 限 公 司 的 主 席，並 於 2003 年
Industrial Co., Ltd. from December 1996 to April 1999 and was awarded         獲佛山市順德區人民政府評為「順德優秀民營企
“Outstanding Private Entrepreneur of Shunde” by Shunde People’s               業家」。黃先生自 2018 年起任佛山市順德區龍江
Government of Foshan in 2003. Mr. Wong has been a permanent                   總商會永遠名譽會長。黃先生乃左笑萍女士的配
honorary president of Foshan Shunde Longjiang General Chamber of              偶及左滿倫先生的姐夫。黃先生為持有本公司控
Commerce since 2018. Mr. Wong is the spouse of Ms. Zuo Xiaoping and           股股東西溪發展及新富星全部已發行股本之信託
the brother-in-law of Mr. Zuo Manlun. Mr. Wong is the founder of a trust      的創辦人。

BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                        董事會成員多元化政策
The Board has adopted a Board diversity policy which sets out the             董事會採納董事會成員多元化政策，該政策載列
approach to achieve diversity on the Board. The Board diversity policy is     達致董事會成員多元化的方法。董事會成員多元
available on the website of the Company.                                      化政策可於本公司網站查閱。

support the attainment of the Company’s strategic objectives and              司策略目標及可持續發展提供支持。本公司藉考
sustainable development. The Company seeks to achieve Board                   慮多項因素，包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化及
diversity through the consideration of a number of factors, including but     教育背景、種族、專業經驗、技能、知識及其他
not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational background,              資格，務求達致董事會成員多元化。本公司決定
ethnicity, professional experience, skills, knowledge and other qualities.    董事會最佳成員組合時，亦將不時考慮其本身業
The Company will also take into consideration its own business model          務模式及具體需要。提名委員會將（視適用情況
and specific needs from time to time in determining the optimal               而定）審閱董事會成員多元化政策，確保其不時
composition of the Board. The Nomination Committee will review the            持續有效。
Board diversity policy, as appropriate, to ensure its continued
effectiveness from time to time.

by: (a) training programs for talents to expedite the readiness and ability   任者：(a) 人才培訓計劃，以加快繼任準備和能力；
for the succession, (b) engagement of recruitment consultant, and (c)         (b) 聘 請 招 聘 顧 問；及 (c) 在 招 募 合 適 管 理 層 候 選

SUPERVISION AND ENHANCEMENT OF RISK                                         風險管理的監督與改進
MANAGEMENT
The Group has set up basic procedures that cover the whole process of       本集團已建立貫穿於整個風險管理基本流程，連
risk management, connecting the channels of communication of risk           接各上下級、各部門和業務單位的風險管理資訊
management information along the reporting lines and among various          溝通管道，確保資訊溝通的及時、準確和完整，
departments and business units to ensure the timely, accurate and           為風險管理監督與改進奠定基礎。
complete communication of information and this has laid the
foundation for the supervision and enhancement of risk management.

The senior management of the Group, focusing on material risks,             本集團的高級管理層以重大風險、重大事件和重
material matters and material decisions, important management               大決策、重要管理及業務流程為重點，對風險管
matters and the business flow, supervises the work relating to the first-   理第一手資訊收集、風險評估、風險管理策略、
hand information collection for risk management, risk assessment, risk      關鍵控制活動及風險管理解決方案的實施情況進
management strategies, critical control activities and the                  行監督。風險管理項目組每年組織對各部門和業
implementation of risk management solutions. The risk management            務單位風險管理工作實施情況和有效性進行一次
project team carries out an annual review and examination on the            年度檢查和檢驗，對風險管理策略進行評估，對

The Company applies the principle of a quiet period in its investor         本公司於其投資者關係溝通上採取緘默期原則。
relations communications. During a quiet period, the Company does not       緘默期期間，本公司不會就市場前景或影響業務

conference call with public in which these matters are discussed. A         分別於年度業績或中期業績披露前 60 天或 30 天

annual results or interim results respectively, and lasts until the

 HKFRS 9 requires that the impairment of financial assets
 should be measured by the “expected credit loss” (“ECL”)
 model. In order to measure the impairment of trade and bills
 receivables and loan receivables under HKFRS 9,
 management should use judgement, assumptions and
 estimation techniques in aspects such as judging significant
 increase in credit risk, estimating the parameters for
 measuring ECLs and determining the forward-looking
 adjustments.
 香港財務報告準則第 9 號規定，金融資產的減值應以「預期
 信貸虧損」     （「預期信貸虧損」）模型計量。為根據香港財務報
 告準則第 9 號計量貿易應收款項及票據和應收借款的減值，
 管理層應於多方面（如就信貸風險大幅增加作出判斷、就計
 量預期信貸虧損的參數作出估計及釐定前瞻性調整）運用判
 斷、假設及估計方法。

financial statements.

                                losses) determined in accordance with generally accepted accounting principles in the PRC to the                                                                至法定儲備，直至儲備金結餘達到其註冊資本的 50% 為止。法定儲備可用
                                statutory reserve until the balance of the reserve fund reaches 50% of its registered capital. The                                                              於抵銷過往年度虧損或增資，惟法定儲備的餘下結餘不得少於註冊資本的

            The consolidated financial statements include the financial                      綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公
            statements of the Company and its subsidiaries for the year                      司截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日止年度的財務
            ended 31 December 2023.                                                          報表。

            A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity),                        附屬公司為本公司直接或間接控制的
            directly or indirectly controlled by the Company. Control is                      實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團承
            achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to                             受或享有參與投資對象業務所得的可
            variable returns from its involvement with the investee and                       變回報，且能透過對投資對象的權力
            has the ability to affect those returns through its power over                   （即 賦 予 本 集 團 現 有 能 力 主 導 投 資 對
            the investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the                       象相 關活 動的既 存權利）影響該等回
            current ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee).               報時，即取得控制權。

2.    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                           2.   會計政策（續）
      2.1   BASIS OF PREPARATION (Continued)                                         2.1   編製基準（續）
            Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                             綜合基準（續）
            Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting                    於一般情況下均存在多數投票權形成
            rights results in control. When the Company has, less than a                   控制權之推定。倘本公司擁有少於投
            majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the                   資對象大多數的投票或類似權利，則
            Group considers all relevant facts and circumstances in                        本集團於評估其是否擁有對投資對象
            assessing whether it has power over an investee, including:                    的權力時，會考慮一切相關事實及情
                                                                                           況，包括：

            (i)      the contractual arrangement with the other vote                       (i)     與投資對象其他投票持有人的
                     holders of the investee;                                                      合約安排；

            (ii)     rights arising from other contractual arrangements;                   (ii)    其 他 合 約 安 排 所 產 生 的 權 利；
                     and                                                                           及

            (iii)    the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                (iii)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

            The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared                      附屬公司的財務報表乃就與本公司相
            for the same reporting period as the Company, using                            同的報告期間採用一致的會計政策編
            consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries                    製。附屬公司的業績由本集團取得控
            are consolidated from the date on which the Group obtains                      制權當日起綜合入賬，並繼續綜合入
            control, and continue to be consolidated until the date that                   賬直至該控制權終止當日為止。
            such control ceases.

            Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive                       損益及其他全面收益的各部分歸屬於
            income are attributed to the owners of the Company and to                      本公司擁有人及非控制權益。即使導
            the non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income                      致非控制權益出現虧絀結餘，附屬公
            of subsidiaries is attributable to the owners of the Company                   司的全面收益總額仍歸屬於本公司擁
            and to the non-controlling interests, even if this results in the              有人及非控制權益。
            non-controlling interests having a deficit balance.

            When necessary, adjustments are made to the financial                          如有需要，附屬公司的財務報表會作
            statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies                  出調整，以使其會計政策與本集團的
            into line with the Group’s accounting policies. All intra-group                會計政策一致。所有與本集團成員公
            assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses and cash                      司之間的交易有關的集團內公司間資
            flows relating to transactions between members of the                          產 及 負 債、權 益、收 益、開 支 及 現 金
            Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                                 流量已在綜合賬目時悉數對銷。

                     HKAS 12 so that it no longer applies to transactions                          則 第 12 號 初 始 確 認 例 外 情 況 的
                     that give rise to equal taxable and deductible                                範 圍，使 其 不 再 適 用 於 產 生 相
                     temporary differences, such as leases and                                     等應課稅及可扣減暫時性差異
                     decommissioning obligations. Therefore, entities are                          的交易，如租賃及棄置義務。因
                     required to recognise a deferred tax asset (provided                          此，實 體 須 就 該 等 交 易 產 生 的
                     that it is probable that sufficient taxable profit will be                    暫時性差異確認遞延稅項資產
                     available) and a deferred tax liability for temporary                        （前提是日後很可能有足夠的應
                     differences arising from these transactions.                                  課稅溢利可供動用）及遞延稅項
                                                                                                   負債。

                     the Group has applied the initial recognition exception                      集團已應用初始確認例外情況，
                     and did not recognise a deferred tax asset and a                             即不確認租賃交易的暫時性差
                     deferred tax liability for temporary differences for                         異產生的遞延所得稅資產和遞
                     transactions related to leases. The Group has applied                        延 所 得 稅 負 債。本 集 團 已 於

                     these amendments, the Group recognised (i) a                                 次 應 用 該 等 修 訂 本 時，本 集 團
                     deferred tax asset for all deductible temporary                              確 認 於 2022 年 1 月 1 日 (i) 與 租 賃

2.    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                         2.   會計政策（續）
      2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                             2.4 重要會計政策
            Investments in associates and joint ventures                             於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
            An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term             聯營公司為本集團擁有一般不少於
            interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting             20% 股本投票權的長期權益的實體，
            rights and over which it has significant influence. Significant          並可對其發揮重大影響力。重大影響
            influence is the power to participate in the financial and               力指參與投資對象的財政及經營決策
            operating policy decisions of the investee, but is not control           之權力，而非控制或共同控制該等決
            or joint control over those policies.                                    策之權力。

            A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the               合營企業為一項合營安排，據此，擁
            parties that have joint control of the arrangement have                  有安排之共同控制權的人士均有權享
            rights to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint control is          有合營企業的淨資產。共同控制乃以
            the contractually agreed sharing of control of an                        合約協定分佔一項安排之控制權，其
            arrangement, which exists only when decisions about the                  僅在相關活動決策必須獲分佔控制權
            relevant activities require unanimous consent of the parties             的人士一致同意時方存在。
            sharing control.

            The Group’s investments in associates and a joint venture                本集團於聯營公司及一間合營企業的
            are stated in the consolidated statement of financial position           投資乃按權益會計法核算，按本集團
            at the Group’s share of net assets under the equity method               應佔淨資產扣除任何減值虧損於綜合
            of accounting, less any impairment losses.                               財務狀況表中呈列。

            The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and other              本集團應佔聯營公司及合營企業收購
            comprehensive income of associates and joint ventures is                 後業績和其他全面收益分別計入損益
            included in profit or loss and other comprehensive income,               及其他全面收益內。此外，倘直接於
            respectively. In addition, when there has been a change                  聯營公司或合營企業的權益確認變動，
            recognised directly in the equity of the associates or joint             則本集團會於綜合權益變動表確認其
            ventures, the Group recognises its share of any changes,                 應佔的任何變動（如適用）。本集團與
            when applicable, in the consolidated statement of changes                其聯營公司或合營企業進行交易而出
            in equity. Unrealised gains and losses resulting from                    現的未實現利益及虧損會按本集團於
            transactions between the Group and its associates or joint               聯營公司或合營企業的權益互相抵銷，
            ventures are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s interests           但如果未實現虧損證明所轉讓資產發
            in the associates or joint ventures, except where unrealised             生減值則除外。收購聯營公司或合營
            losses provide evidence of an impairment of the asset                    企業產生的商譽計入本集團於聯營公
            transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition of                    司或合營企業的投資的一部分。
            associates or joint ventures is included as part of the Group’s
            investments in associates or joint ventures.

2.    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                              2.   會計政策（續）
      2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                      2.4 重要會計政策（續）
            Interest in joint operation                                                   於合營業務之權益
            A joint operation is a joint arrangement whereby the parties                  合營業務為共同安排，據此，擁有該
            that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the                 安排之共同控制權的各方有權享有與
            assets, and obligations for the liabilities, relating to the                  該安排有關的資產及就負債承擔責任。
            arrangement. Joint control is the contractually agreed                        共同控制乃以合約協定分佔一項安排
            sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists only                       之控制權，其僅在相關活動決策必須
            when decisions about the relevant activities require the                      獲分佔控制權的人士一致同意時方存
            unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.                             在。

            The Group recognises in relation to its interest in a joint                   本集團就其於合營業務之權益確認：
            operation:

            •        its assets, including its share of any assets held jointly;          •   其 資 產，包 括 分 佔 任 何 共 同 持
                                                                                              有之資產；

            •        its liabilities, including its share of any liabilities              •   其 負 債，包 括 分 佔 任 何 共 同 產
                     incurred jointly;                                                        生之負債；

            •        its revenue from the sale of its share of the output                 •   出售其於合營業務應佔產出之
                     arising from the joint operation;                                        所得收入；

            •        its share of the revenue from the sale of the output by              •   出售合營業務產出之應佔收入；
                     the joint operation; and                                                 及

            •        its expenses, including its share of any expenses                    •   其 開 支，包 括 分 佔 任 何 共 同 產
                     incurred jointly.                                                        生之開支。

            The assets, liabilities, revenues and expenses relating to the                本集團於合營業務之權益之有關資產、
            Group’s interest in a joint operation are accounted for in                    負債、收入及開支乃根據適用於相關
            accordance with the HKFRSs applicable to the particular                       資產、負債、收入及開支之香港財務
            assets, liabilities, revenues and expenses.                                   報告準則入賬。

2.    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                           2.   會計政策（續）
      2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                   2.4 重要會計政策（續）
            Business combinations and goodwill                                         業務合併及商譽
            Business combinations are accounted for using the                          業務合併乃以購買法入賬。轉讓之代
            acquisition method. The consideration transferred is                       價乃以收購日期的公允價值計算，該
            measured at the acquisition-date fair value which is the sum               公允價值為本集團轉讓的資產於收購
            of the acquisition-date fair values of assets transferred by               日期的公允價值、本集團自被收購方
            the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group to the former                  之前度擁有人承擔的負債，及本集團
            owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued by                  發行以換取被收購方控制權的股本權
            the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree. For each                益的總和。於各業務合併中，本集團
            business combination, the Group elects whether to measure                  選擇是否以公允價值或被收購方可識
            the non-controlling interests in the acquiree at fair value or             別資產淨值的應佔比例，計算被收購
            at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable net              方的非控制權益。非控制權益的一切
            assets. All other components of non-controlling interests are              其他成分乃按公允價值計量。收購相
            measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are                      關成本於產生時列為開支。
            expensed as incurred.

            The Group determines that it has acquired a business when                  當所取得的一組活動及資產包括一項
            the acquired set of activities and assets includes an input                投入和一項重要過程，而兩者共同對
            and a substantive process that together significantly                      形成產出的能力有重大貢獻，則本集
            contribute to the ability to create outputs.                               團釐定為業務收購。

            When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                        當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合
            financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate                   約條款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相
            classification and designation in accordance with the                      關條件，評估將承接的金融資產及負
            contractual terms, economic circumstances and pertinent                    債，以作出適當的分類及標示，其中
            conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the                   包括分離被收購方主合約中的嵌入式
            separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the                衍生工具。
            acquiree.

            If the business combination is achieved in stages, the                     倘業務合併分階段進行，以往持有的
            previously held equity interest is remeasured at its                       股本權益會以收購日期的公允價值重
            acquisition date fair value and any resulting gain or loss is              新計量，任何因此帶來的利益或虧損
            recognised in profit or loss.                                              於損益中確認。

            Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the                      將由收購方轉讓的任何或然代價於收
            acquirer is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date.              購日期按公允價值確認。或然代價（分
            Contingent consideration classified as an asset or liability is            類 為 資 產 或 負 債）按 公 允 價 值 計 量，
            measured at fair value with changes in fair value recognised               而公允價值變動則於損益確認。分類
            in profit or loss. Contingent consideration that is classified as          為權益之或然代價並不會重新計量，
            equity is not remeasured and subsequent settlement is                      其後結算於權益中入賬。
            accounted for within equity.

            Business combinations and goodwill (Continued)                            業務合併及商譽（續）
            Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the excess of the           商譽初始按成本計量，即已轉讓代價、
            aggregate of the consideration transferred, the amount                    已確認非控制權益金額及本集團先前
            recognised for non-controlling interests and any fair value of            持有被收購方股本權益的任何公允價
            the Group’s previously held equity interests in the acquiree              值之總額超出所收購可識別資產及所
            over the identifiable assets acquired and liabilities assumed.            承擔負債的差額。倘此代價及其他項
            If the sum of this consideration and other items is lower than            目的總和低於所收購淨資產的公允價
            the fair value of the net assets acquired, the difference is,             值，則再評估後的差額於損益確認為
            after reassessment, recognised in profit or loss as a gain on             議價收購所得利益。
            bargain purchase.

            After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less any          於初始確認後，商譽按成本減任何累
            accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for                     計減值虧損計量。商譽須每年進行減
            impairment annually or more frequently if events or changes               值測試，若有事件發生或情況改變顯
            in circumstances indicate that the carrying value may be                  示賬面值可能減值，則會更頻密地進
            impaired. The Group will perform its annual impairment test               行 測 試。本 集 團 將 於 12 月 31 日 進 行 商
            of goodwill as at 31 December. For the purpose of                         譽的年度減值測試。為進行減值測試，
            impairment testing, goodwill acquired in a business                       無論本集團其他資產或負債是否已分
            combination is, from the acquisition date, allocated to each              配至現金產生單位或現金產生單位組
            of the Group’s cash-generating units, or groups of cash-                  別，因業務合併而購入的商譽自收購
            generating units, that are expected to benefit from the                   日期起分配至預期可從合併產生的協
            synergies of the combination, irrespective of whether other               同效益中獲益的本集團各現金產生單
            assets or liabilities of the Group are assigned to those units            位或現金產生單位組別。
            or groups of units.

            Impairment is determined by assessing the recoverable                     減值乃通過評估與商譽有關的現金產
            amount of the cash-generating unit (group of cash-                        生單位（現金產生單位組別）的可收回
            generating units) to which the goodwill relates. Where the                金 額 釐 定。當 現 金 產 生 單 位（現 金 產
            recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit (group of                  生單 位組 別）的 可收 回金額低於賬 面
            cash-generating units) is less than the carrying amount, an               值時，則確認減值虧損。已確認商譽
            impairment loss is recognised. An impairment loss                         減值虧損不得於其後期間撥回。
            recognised for goodwill is not reversed in a subsequent
            period.

            unit (or group of cash-generating units) and part of the                  金產 生單 位組別）而 該單位的部分 業

2.    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                             2.   會計政策（續）
      2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                     2.4 重要會計政策（續）
            Fair value measurement                                                       公允價值之計量
            The Group measures its investment properties, asset held                     本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計量
            for sale, other financial assets and other financial liabilities at          其投資物業、持作出售資產、其他金
            fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is                融資產及其他金融負債。公允價值為
            the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to                 市場參與者於計量日期在有序交易中
            transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market                出售資產所收取的價格或轉讓負債所
            participants at the measurement date. The fair value                         支付的價格。公允價值計量乃假設出
            measurement is based on the presumption that the                             售資產或轉讓負債的交易於資產或負
            transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes                債主要市場或（在無主要市場情況下）
            place either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or          最具優勢市場進行而作出。主要或最
            in the absence of a principal market, in the most                            具優勢市場須為本集團可進入之市場。
            advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal                資產或負債的公允價值乃基於市場參
            or the most advantageous market must be accessible by                        與者於資產或負債定價所用的假設計
            the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured             量（即假 設市 場參與者會 以最佳經濟
            using the assumptions that market participants would use                     利益行事）。
            when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market
            participants act in their economic best interest.

            A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                 非金融資產的公允價值計量須計及市
            account a market participant’s ability to generate economic                  場參與者透過最大限度使用該資產達
            benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by                致最佳用途，或將該資產出售予將最
            selling it to another market participant that would use the                  大限度使用該資產達致最佳用途的其
            asset in its highest and best use.                                           他市場參與者以產生經濟利益的能力。

            The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                  本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備充
            the circumstances and for which sufficient data are                          分數據以供計量公允價值的估值方法，
            available to measure fair value, maximising the use of                       以盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡
            relevant observable inputs and minimising the use of                         量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
            unobservable inputs.

            For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the                        就按經常性基準於本綜合財務報表確

            than inventories, contract assets, deferred tax assets and                   延 稅 項 資 產 及 投 資 物 業 除 外），則 會

            Property, plant and equipment and depreciation                            物業、廠房及設備之折舊
            Property, plant and equipment, other than construction in                 除在建工程外，物業、廠房及設備按
            progress, are stated at cost less accumulated depreciation                成本減累計折舊及任何減值虧損列賬。
            and any impairment losses. The cost of an item of property,               物業、廠房及設備項目的成本包括其
            plant and equipment comprises its purchase price and any                  購買價及使資產處於擬定用途的運作
            directly attributable costs of bringing the asset to its working          狀況及地點而產生的任何直接成本。
            condition and location for its intended use.

            Expenditure incurred after items of property, plant and                   物業、廠房及設備項目投入運作後產
            equipment have been put into operation, such as repairs                   生的支出（例如維修及保養），一般於
            and maintenance, is normally charged to profit or loss in the             其產生期間自損益內扣除。倘能夠符
            period in which it is incurred. In situations where the                   合確認標準，重大檢查的開支會於資
            recognition criteria are satisfied, the expenditure for a major           產賬面值中資本化為替換。倘物業、
            inspection is capitalised in the carrying amount of the asset             廠房及設備的主要部分須定期替換，
            as a replacement. Where significant parts of property, plant              本集團會確認該等部分為有特定使用
            and equipment are required to be replaced at intervals, the               年期的個別資產及相應計提折舊。
            Group recognises such parts as individual assets with
            specific useful lives and depreciates them accordingly.

            Depreciation is calculated on the straight-line basis and the             折舊以直線法及產量法計算，按每項
            units of production basis to write off the cost of each item of           物業、廠房及設備項目的估計使用年
            property, plant and equipment to its residual value over its              期撇銷其成本至其剩餘價值。為此目
            estimated useful life. The principal annual rates used for this           的而主要使用的每年折舊率如下：
            purpose are as follows:

            Freehold land                   Not depreciated                           永久業權土地       並無折舊
            Land and buildings              2.0% to 5.0%                              土地及樓宇        2.0% 至 5.0%
            Plant and machinery             9.5% to 20.0% or units of                 廠房及機器        9.5% 至 20.0% 或
                                               production basis                                       按產量法
            Furniture, fixtures and         9.5% to 32.3%                             家具、裝置及       9.5% 至 32.3%
              office equipment                                                         辦公室設備
            Vehicles                        9.5% to 32.3%                             運輸工具         9.5% 至 32.3%
            Leasehold improvements          Over the shorter of the lease             租賃物業裝修       按租期與 20.0%
                                              terms and 20.0%                                        兩者中的較短者

            Where parts of an item of property, plant and equipment                   倘物業、廠房及設備項目的部分有不
            have different useful lives, the cost of that item is allocated           同的可使用年期，則該項目的成本須
            on a reasonable basis among the parts and each part is                    在各部分之間合理分配，而各部分須
            depreciated separately. Residual values, useful lives and the             單獨計算折舊。剩餘價值、可使用年
            depreciation method are reviewed, and adjusted if                         期和折舊方法至少於各財政年度末檢
            appropriate, at least at each financial year end.                         討一次，並在適當情況下作出調整。

            Non-current assets and disposal groups held for sale                       非流動資產及持作出售之出售組別
            Non-current assets and disposal groups are classified as                   倘非流動資產及出售組別的賬面值將
            held for sale if their carrying amounts will be recovered                  主要透過出售交易而非持續使用來收

            customary for the sale of such assets or disposal groups                   出售，且出售的可能性很高。不論本
            and its sale must be highly probable. All assets and liabilities           集團於出售後是否保留其前附屬公司
            of a subsidiary classified as a disposal group are reclassified            的非控股權益，分類為出售組別的附
            as held for sale regardless of whether the Group retains a                 屬公司的所有資產及負債均重新分類
            non-controlling interest in its former subsidiary after the sale.          為持作出售。

            Leases                                                                       租賃
            The Group assesses at contract inception whether a                           本集團於合約開始時評估合約是否屬
            contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains, a             於或包含租賃。倘合約將可識別資產
            lease if the contract conveys the right to control the use of                於一段時間內之使用控制權有償轉讓，
            an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for                     則該合約為租賃或包含租賃。
            consideration.

            Group as a lessee                                                            本集團作為承租人
            The Group applies a single recognition and measurement                       本集團就所有租賃應用單一確認及計
            approach for all leases, except for short-term leases and                    量方法，惟短期租賃及低價值資產租
            leases of low-value assets. The Group recognises lease                       賃除外。本集團確認作出租賃付款的
            liabilities to make lease payments and right-of-use assets                   租賃 負債 及使用 權資產（即使用相關
            representing the right to use the underlying assets.                         資產的權利）。

                     underlying asset is available for use). Right-of-use                      使用權資產按成本減任何累計

                     depreciation and any impairment losses, and adjusted                      就任何重新計量租賃負債作出
                     for any remeasurement of lease liabilities. The cost of                   調 整。使 用 權 資 產 的 成 本 包 括
                     right-of-use assets includes the amount of lease                          已 確 認 之 租 賃 負 債 金 額、已 產
                     liabilities recognised, initial direct costs incurred, and                生之初始直接成本以及於開始
                     lease payments made at or before the                                      日期或之前作出的租賃付款減
                     commencement date less any lease incentives                               已 收 取 之 任 何 租 賃 優 惠。使 用
                     received. The cost of a right-of-use asset also includes                  權資產的成本亦包括拆卸及移
                     an estimate of costs to dismantle and remove the                          除 相 關 資 產，或 還 原 相 關 資 產
                     underlying asset or to restore the underlying asset or                    或 其 所 在 地 產 生 的 估 計 成 本。
                     the site on which it is located. Right-of-use assets are                  使用權資產於租期及資產的估
                     depreciated on a straight-line basis over the shorter of                  計 可 使 用 年 期（以 較 短 者 為 準）
                     the lease terms and the estimated useful lives of the                     內按直線法折舊，如下：
                     assets as follows:

                     Leasehold land                        10 to 50 years                      租賃業權土地          10 至 50 年

                     Other equipment                       1 to 5 years                        其他設備            1至5年

                     If ownership of the leased asset transfers to the Group                   倘租賃資產的擁有權於租期結

                     calculated using the estimated useful life of the asset.                  估計可使用年期計算折舊。

            When the Group acts as a lessor, it classifies at lease                 當本集團作為出租人，則於租賃開始
            inception (or when there is a lease modification) each of its           時（或出現租賃修訂時）將其每項租賃
            leases as either an operating lease or a finance lease.                 分為經營租賃或融資租賃。

            Leases in which the Group does not transfer substantially all           如本集團並未轉移資產擁有權附帶之
            the risks and rewards incidental to ownership of an asset               絕大部分風險及回報之租賃，均分類
            are classified as operating leases. When a contract contains            為經營租賃。如合約包含租賃和非租
            lease and non-lease components, the Group allocates the                 賃部分，本集團按相對獨立售價基準
            consideration in the contract to each component on a                    將合約代價分配至各個部分。租金收
            relative stand-alone selling price basis. Rental income is              益於租賃期按直線法入賬並由於其經
            accounted for on a straight-line basis over the lease terms             營性質於損益內計入收入。磋商及安
            and is included in revenue in profit or loss due to its                 排經營租賃所產生之初始直接成本會
            operating nature. Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating          加入租賃資產賬面值，並按與租金收
            and arranging an operating lease are added to the carrying              益相同之基準於租賃期內確認。或然
            amount of the leased asset and recognised over the lease                租金於賺取期間確認為收入。
            term on the same basis as rental income. Contingent rents
            are recognised as revenue in the period in which they are
            earned.

            Leases that transfer substantially all the risks and rewards            凡將相關資產的擁有權附帶的絕大部
            incidental to ownership of an underlying asset to the lessee            分風險與回報轉讓至承租人的租賃均
            are accounted for as finance leases.                                    列為融資租賃。

            When the Group is an intermediate lessor, a sublease is                 如本集團為出租中介，則參考總租賃
            classified as a finance lease or operating lease with                   產生的使用權資產將轉租賃分類為融
            reference to the right-of-use asset arising from the head               資租賃或經營租賃。如總租賃為本集
            lease. If the head lease is a short-term lease to which the             團採用資產負債表內確認豁免的短期
            Group applies the on-balance sheet recognition exemption,               租賃，則本集團將轉租賃分類為經營
            the Group classifies the sublease as an operating lease.                租賃。

            Impairment of financial assets                                            金融資產減值
            The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit                     本集團就並非按公允價值計入損益持
            losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at FVTPL.               有的所有債務工具確認預期信貸虧損
            ECLs are based on the difference between the contractual                 （「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信貸虧
            cash flows due in accordance with the contract and all the                損乃基於根據合約應付的合約現金流
            cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at               量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量
            an approximation of the original effective interest rate. The             之間的差額，並按接近原有實際利率
            expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of              貼現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持
            collateral held or other credit enhancements that are                     抵押品或合約條款所包含的其他信貸
            integral to the contractual terms.                                        增強措施所得的現金流量。

            General approach                                                         一般方法
            ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for              預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自
            which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk           初始確認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加
            since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses           的信貸風險而言，會為未來 12 個月內
            that result from default events that are possible within the             的可能違約事件所產生的信貸虧損計
            next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures              提 預 期 信 貸 虧 損（12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧
            for which there has been a significant increase in credit risk           損）。就 自 初 始 確 認 以 來 信 貸 風 險 大
            since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for              幅增加的信貸風險而言，不論違約時
            credit losses expected over the remaining life of the                    間如何，須就預期於風險餘下存續期
            exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a lifetime          內的 信貸 虧損計 提虧 損撥備（整個 存
            ECL).                                                                    續期預期信貸虧損）。

            At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the                   於各報告日期，本集團評估自初始確
            credit risk on a financial instrument has increased                      認後金融工具的信貸風險是否大幅增
            significantly since initial recognition. When making the                 加。於進行評估時，本集團將於報告
            assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default                     日期金融工具發生違約的風險與初始
            occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting                確認日期金融工具發生違約的風險進
            date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial               行比較，並考慮所有毋須付出不必要
            instrument as at the date of initial recognition and considers           成本或努力即可獲得的合理支持資料，
            reasonable and supportable information that is available                 包括歷史及前瞻性資料。當合約付款
            without undue cost or effort, including historical and forward-          已逾期超過 30 天，本集團認為信貸風
            looking information. The Group considers that there has                  險已大幅增加。
            been a significant increase in credit risk when contractual
            payments are more than 30 days past due.

            The Group considers a financial asset in default when                    當合約付款已逾期 90 天，本集團認為
            contractual payments are 90 days past due. However, in                   金融資產將會違約。可是，於某些情
            certain cases, the Group may also consider a financial asset             況下，倘內部或外部資料顯示在並無
            to be in default when internal or external information                   計及本集團持有的任何信貸增強措施
            indicates that the Group is unlikely to receive the                      前，本集團不大可能悉數收到未償還
            outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking into               合約款項，本集團亦可能認為金融資
            account any credit enhancements held by the Group.                       產將會違約。

2.    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                        2.   會計政策（續）
      2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                2.4 重要會計政策（續）
            Impairment of financial assets (Continued)                              金融資產減值（續）
            General approach (Continued)                                            一般方法（續）
            For debt instruments at fair value through other                        就按公允價值計入其他全面收益的債
            comprehensive income, the Group applies the low credit risk             務工具而言，本集團應用低信貸風險
            simplification. At each reporting date, the Group evaluates             簡化法。於各報告日期，本集團使用
            whether the debt instruments are considered to have low                 所有毋須付出不必要成本或努力即可
            credit risk using all reasonable and supportable information            獲得的合理支持資料，評估債務工具
            that is available without undue cost or effort. In making that          是否被視為擁有低信貸風險。在進行

            investments. It is the Group’s policy to measure ECLs on                本集團的政策是以 12 個月為基礎衡量
            such instruments on a 12-month basis. However, when there               此類工具的預期信貸虧損。然而，當

            Simplified approach                                                            簡化方法
            For trade and bills receivables and contract assets that do                    就並無包含重大融資成分或本集團應

            Group applies the practical expedient of not adjusting the                     影響的貿易應收款項及票據和合約資

            the simplified approach, the Group does not track changes                      並無追蹤信貸風險的變化，而於各報

            credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors                   務人及經濟環境的特定前瞻性因素作
            specific to the debtors and the economic environment.                          出調整。

            For trade and bills receivables that contain a significant                      就包含重大融資成分的貿易應收款項

            The subsequent measurement of financial liabilities                        金融負債的其後計量視乎下述分類而
            depends on their classification, as described below:                       定：

            Financial liabilities at fair value through profit or loss                 按公允價值計入損益的金融負債

            recognition as at FVTPL.                                                   指定為按公允價值計入損益的金融負
                                                                                       債。

            Financial liabilities are classified as held for trading if they           倘為於近期購回而產生金融負債，則
            are incurred for the purpose of repurchasing in the near                   金融負債分類為持作買賣。此類別亦
            term. This category also includes derivative financial                     包括本集團所訂立於香港財務報告準
            instruments entered into by the Group that are not                         則第 9 號所界定的對沖關係中並無指
            designated as hedging instruments in hedge relationships                   定為對沖工具的衍生金融工具。獨立
            as defined by HKFRS 9. Separated embedded derivatives                      嵌入式衍生工具亦分類為持作買賣，
            are also classified as held for trading unless they are                    惟指定為有效對沖工具的衍生工具則
            designated as effective hedging instruments.                               除外。

            Gains or losses on liabilities held for trading are recognised             持作買賣的負債利益或虧損確認於損
            in profit or loss. The net fair value gain or loss recognised in           益。已於損益確認的公允價值淨利益
            profit or loss does not include any interest charged on these              或淨虧損並不包括任何該金融負債的
            financial liabilities.                                                     利息。

            FVTPL are designated at the date of initial recognition and                損益的金融負債於初始確認日期指定，
            only if the criteria in HKFRS 9 are satisfied.                             並僅於符合香港財務報告準則第 9 號
                                                                                       標準的情況下方會進行。

            Financial liabilities at amortised cost (trade and bills                   按攤 銷成 本列賬 的金融負債（貿易應
            payables, other payables and borrowings)                                   付款項及票據、其他應付款項及借款）
            After initial recognition, trade and bills payables, other                 於初始確認後，貿易應付款項及票據、
            payables, and interest-bearing borrowings are subsequently                 其他應付款項及計息借款其後以實際

            (“EIR”) method. Gains and losses are recognised in profit or               量。終止確認負債及於實際利率攤銷
            loss when the liabilities are derecognised as well as through              過程中產生的利益及虧損於損益確認。
            the EIR amortisation process.

            included as finance costs in profit or loss.                               入於損益內的融資成本。

            Derecognition of financial liabilities                                     終止確認金融負債
            A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation                  金融負債於負債責任獲解除、取消或
            under the liability is discharged or cancelled or expires.                 期滿時終止確認。

            When an existing financial liability is replaced by another                當現有金融負債由同一貸款人所提供
            from the same lender on substantially different terms, or the              條款差異重大的另一金融負債取代，
            terms of an existing liability are substantially modified, such            或現有負債的條款已作重大修訂，則
            an exchange or modification is treated as the derecognition                有關取代或修訂被視為終止確認原有

            The difference in the respective carrying amounts is                       額於損益確認。
            recognised in profit or loss.

            Offsetting of financial instruments                                        抵銷金融工具
            Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the              倘若現時存在可強制執行的法定權利
            net amount is reported in the consolidated statement of                    以抵銷已確認金額，且亦有意以淨額
            financial position if there is a currently enforceable legal               結算，或同時變現資產及償還負債，
            right to offset the recognised amounts and there is an                     則金融資產及金融負債可互相抵銷，
            intention to settle on a net basis, or to realise the assets and           並將淨額於綜合財務狀況表內呈報。
            settle the liabilities simultaneously.

            timing of the recognition in profit or loss depends on the                 認之時間。

            Provisions                                                               撥備
            A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or            倘因 過往 事件導 致現 有債務（法定 或
            constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is           推定）及 日後可 能需 要有資源流出 以
            probable that a future outflow of resources will be required             償還債務，則確認撥備，但必須能可
            to settle the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can          靠估計有關債務金額。當本集團預期
            be made of the amount of the obligation. When the Group                  部份或全部撥備可獲償付時，如根據
            expects some or all of a provision to be reimbursed, for                 一項保險合約，有關償付額僅可於實
            example, under an insurance contract, the reimbursement                  際確定時方可確認為獨立資產。有關
            is recognised as a separate asset, but only when the                     撥備的開支於扣除任何償付後於收益
            reimbursement is virtually certain. The expense relating to a            表呈列。
            provision is presented in the statement of profit or loss net
            of any reimbursement.

            When the effect of discounting is material, the amount                   倘折現的影響重大，則就撥備確認的
            recognised for a provision is the present value at the end of            金額為預期需用作償還債務的未來支
            the reporting period of the future expenditures expected to              出於報告期末的現值。因時間流逝而
            be required to settle the obligation. The increase in the                產生的折現現值增額會計入損益內的
            discounted present value amount arising from the passage                 融資成本。
            of time is included in finance costs in profit or loss.

            The Group provides for warranties in relation to the sale of             於保修期間，本集團就銷售若干工業
            certain industrial products and the provision of construction            產品及提供建築服務發生的缺陷提供
            services for general repairs of defects occurring during the             一般維修的擔保。就本集團提供該等
            warranty period. Provisions for these assurance-type                     保證型擔保而作出的撥備乃根據銷量
            warranties granted by the Group are initially recognised                 以及過往維修及退貨程度經驗初步確
            based on sales volume and past experience of the level of                認，並貼現至其現值（如適當）。擔保
            repairs and returns, discounted to their present values as               相關成本每年予以修訂。
            appropriate. The warranty-related cost is revised annually.

            Income tax                                                                所得稅
            Income tax comprises current and deferred tax. Income tax                 所得稅包括即期及遞延稅項。與並非
            relating to items recognised outside profit or loss is                    於損益確認的項目有關的所得稅會在
            recognised outside profit or loss, either in other                        並非損益的其他全面收益內或直接在
            comprehensive income or directly in equity.                               權益內確認。

            Current tax assets and liabilities are measured at the                    即期稅項資產及負債按預期獲稅務機
            amount expected to be recovered from or paid to the                       構退回或向稅務機構支付的款項計量。
            taxation authorities, based on tax rates (and tax laws) that              計量的基準為報告期末已實行或已實
            have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of                  際執行的稅率（及稅法），亦考慮本集
            the reporting period, taking into consideration                           團經營所在國家現行的詮釋及慣例。
            interpretations and practices prevailing in the countries in
            which the Group operates.

            Deferred tax is provided, using the liability method, on all              遞延稅項乃採用負債法就報告期末資
            temporary differences at the end of the reporting period                  產及負債的稅基與其用作財務呈報的
            between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their                 賬面值之間的所有暫時差額作出撥備，
            carrying amounts for financial reporting purposes, except                 惟不就支柱二所得稅確認遞延稅項。
            that deferred tax is not recognised for the Pillar Two income
            taxes.

            Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for all taxable                   遞延稅項負債乃就所有應課稅暫時差
            temporary differences, except:                                            額而確認，惟下列情況除外：

            •        when the deferred tax liability arises from the initial          •   在交易時不影響會計溢利或應

            Share-based payments                                                        以股份為基礎的支付
            The Company operates a share option scheme and a share                      本公司設有一項購股權計劃及一項股
            award scheme. Employees (including directors) of the Group                  份獎勵計劃。本集團僱員（包括董事）
            receive remuneration in the form of share-based payments,                   以股份為基礎的支付形式收取薪酬，
            whereby employees render services in exchange for equity                    而僱員則提供服務以換取股本工具
            instruments (“equity-settled transactions”).                               （「以股本付款交易」）。

            The cost of equity-settled transactions is measured by                     以股本付款交易的成本乃根據授出當
            reference to the fair value at the date at which they are                  日的公允價值計算。
            granted.

            The cost of equity-settled transactions is recognised in                   以股本付款交易的成本連同相關權益
            employee benefit expense, together with a corresponding                    的增加於達成表現及╱或服務條件時
            increase in equity, over the period in which the performance               在僱員福利開支內確認。於各報告期
            and/or service conditions are fulfilled. The cumulative                    末就以股本付款交易確認累計開支，
            expense recognised for equity-settled transactions at the                  直至歸屬日期為止，反映歸屬期屆滿
            end of each reporting period until the vesting date reflects               部分以及本集團對最終將歸屬的股本
            the extent to which the vesting period has expired and the                 工具數目的最佳估計。期間在損益扣
            Group’s best estimate of the number of equity instruments                  除或入賬的金額指期初及期末已確認
            that will ultimately vest. The charge or credit to profit or loss          累計開支的變動。
            for a period represents the movement in the cumulative
            expense recognised as at the beginning and end of that
            period.

            Service and non-market performance conditions are not                      釐定獎勵於授出日期的公允價值時，
            taken into account when determining the grant date fair                    不會考慮服務及非市場表現條件，但
            value of awards, but the likelihood of the conditions being                會評估達成該等條件的可能性，作為
            met is assessed as part of the Group’s best estimate of the                本集團對最終將歸屬的股本工具數目
            number of equity instruments that will ultimately vest. Market             的最佳估計。市場表現條件反映於授
            performance conditions are reflected within the grant date                 出日期的公允價值內。獎勵所附帶但
            fair value. Any other conditions attached to an award, but                 並無相關服務要求的任何其他條件視
            without an associated service requirement, are considered                  為非歸屬條件。除非有服務及╱或表
            to be non-vesting conditions. Non-vesting conditions are                   現條件，否則非歸屬條件反映於獎勵
            reflected in the fair value of an award and lead to an                     的公允價值內，並將即時支銷獎勵。
            immediate expensing of an award unless there are also
            service and/or performance conditions.

            For awards that do not ultimately vest because non-market                  因未能達成非市場表現及╱或服務條
            performance and/or service conditions have not been met,                   件而最終並無歸屬的獎勵不會確認開
            no expense is recognised. Where awards include a market                    支。倘獎勵包括市場或非歸屬條件，
            or non-vesting condition, the transactions are treated as                  不論市場或非歸屬條件是否達成，交
            vesting irrespective of whether the market or non-vesting                  易視為歸屬，惟所有其他表現及╱或
            condition is satisfied, provided that all other performance                服務條件須已達成。
            and/or service conditions are satisfied.

            Foreign currencies                                                        外幣
            The Company’s functional currency is Hong Kong dollars                    本公司的功能貨幣為港元，而本公司
            while the Company’s presentation currency for the                         綜合財務報表的呈列貨幣則為人民幣。
            consolidated financial statements is Renminbi. In preparing               於編製各個別集團實體之財務報表時，

            currency (foreign currencies) are recognised at the rates of              認。於各報告期末，以外幣列值之貨

            end of each reporting period, monetary items denominated                  以外幣計值的按公允價值計量之非貨
            in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing            幣項目，按釐定公允價值當日之現行
            at that date. Non-monetary items carried at fair value that               匯率重新換算。以外幣按歷史成本計
            are denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at                 算之非貨幣項目不會重新換算。
            the rates prevailing at the date when the fair value was
            determined. Non-monetary items that are measured in
            terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are not
            retranslated.

                     interest costs on those foreign currency borrowings;

                     order to hedge certain foreign currency risks; and                   之交易之匯兌差額；及

            •        exchange differences on monetary items receivable                •    應收或應付一項海外業務之貨

                     settlement is neither planned nor likely to occur                     算，發 生 結 算 之 可 能 性 亦 不 大
                     (therefore forming part of the net investment in the                 （因此構成海外業務投資淨額之

       Fair value of investment properties                                            投資物業公允價值
       The best evidence of fair value is current prices in an active                 公允價值之最佳憑證為相同地點及狀況之
       market for properties in the same location and condition and                   物 業 當 時 於 活 躍 市 場 之 價 格，並 受 類 似 租
       subject to similar leases and other contracts. In the absence of               約 及 其 他 合 約 規 限。在 欠 缺 該 等 資 料 之 情
       such information, management determines the amount within a                    況 下，管 理 層 根 據 合 理 公 允 價 值 估 計 範 圍
       range of reasonable fair value estimates. In making its judgement,             釐 定 有 關 金 額。在 作 出 判 斷 時，管 理 層 會
       management considers information from a variety of sources,                    考慮不同來源之資料，包括：(i) 獨立估值；
       including (i) independent valuations; (ii) current prices in an active         (ii) 性質、狀況及地點不同（或受不同租約或
       market for properties of a different nature, condition and location            其他合約規限）之物業當時於活躍市場之價
       (or subject to different leases or other contracts), adjusted to               格，並 加 以 調 整 以 反 映 有 關 差 異；(iii) 類 似
       reflect those differences; (iii) recent prices of similar properties in        物業於較不活躍市場之近期價格（或會作出
       less active markets, with adjustments to reflect any changes in                調 整，以 反 映 自 按 交 易 之 日 以 來 經 濟 狀 況
       economic conditions since the dates of transactions that                       之任何轉變對價格之影響）；及 (iv) 根據對未
       occurred at those prices; and (iv) discounted cash flow projections,           來現金流量之可靠估計而預測之貼現現金
       based on reliable estimates of future cash flows, derived from the             流 量，輔 以 任 何 現 有 租 約 與 其 他 合 約 之 條
       terms of any existing lease and other contracts, and (where                    款及（如可能）外在憑證（如地點及狀況相同
       possible) from external evidence such as current market rates for              之類似物業之當時市場租金），並採用反映
       similar properties in the same location and condition, and using               當時市場對現金流量金額及時間之不確定
       discount rates that reflect current market assessments of the                  性評估之貼現率計算。
       uncertainty in the amount and timing of cash flows.

       Leases — Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                             租賃 — 估算增量借款利率
       The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a             本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利率，
       lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”)            因此使用增量借款利率（「增量借款利率」）
       to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the         計 量 租 賃 負 債。增 量 借 款 利 率 為 本 集 團 於
       Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a              類似經濟環境中為取得與使用權資產價值
       similar security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar          相 近 之 資 產，而 以 類 似 借 期 與 類 似 抵 押 品
       value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment.             借 入 所 需 資 金 應 支 付 之 利 率。因 此，增 量
       The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would have to pay”,                 借款利率反映了本集團「應支付」的款項，
       which requires estimation when no observable rates are available               當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂立融資交
       (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into financing                     易之附屬公司而言）或當須對利率進行調整
       transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to reflect the terms             以反映租賃之條款及條件時（如當租賃並非
       and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases are not in               以附屬公司之功能貨幣訂立時），則須對利
       the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group estimates the IBR             率作出估計。當可觀察輸入數據（如市場利
       using observable inputs (such as market interest rates) when                   率）可 用 時，本 集 團 用 此 估 算 增 量 借 款 利
       available and is required to make certain entity-specific estimates            率，並須作出若干實體特定的估計（如附屬
       (such as the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).                          公司的獨立信貸評級）。

5.    REVENUE, OTHER REVENUE, INCOME AND                                      5.   收入、其他收入、收益及利益（續）
      GAINS (Continued)
      REVENUE (Continued)                                                          收入（續）
      Performance obligations                                                      履約責任
      Information about the Group’s performance obligations is                     有關本集團履約責任的資料概述如下：
      summarised below:

      Sale of goods                                                                銷售貨品
      The performance obligation is satisfied upon delivery of the goods           本 集 團 於 交 貨 時 履 行 責 任，付 款 一 般 在 交
      and payment is generally due within 30 to 360 days from delivery             貨 後 30 至 360 天 內 到 期，或 視 乎 市 況、市
      or is settled on an advance receipt basis depending on the market            埸策略及與客戶的關係按預收方式結算。
      conditions, market tactics and relationships with the customers.

      Renovation, installation and engineering services                            裝修、安裝及工程服務
      The performance obligation is satisfied over time as services are            履約責任隨提供服務而隨時間推移履行，
      rendered and payment is generally due within 30 days from the                且付款一般於結算日起 30 天內到期。客戶
      date of billing. A certain percentage of payment is retained by              保留一定百分比的付款直至保留期結束，
      customers until the end of the retention period as the Group’s               乃由於本集團獲取最終付款的權利視乎於
      entitlement to the final payment is conditional on the satisfaction          客戶按合約規定的一段時間內對服務質素
      of the service quality by the customers over a certain period as             表示滿意後方可作實。
      stipulated in the contracts.

      Logistics and other related services                                         物流及其他相關服務
      The performance obligation is satisfied over time as services are            履約責任隨著提供服務的時間推移履行，
      rendered and payment is generally due within 30 days upon                    且付款一般於服務完成後 30 天內到期或視
      completion of services or is settled on an advance receipt basis             乎與客戶的關係按預收方式結算。
      depending on the relationships with the customers.

      The amounts of transaction prices allocated to the remaining                 於報告期末，分配至餘下履約責任（未履行
      performance obligations (unsatisfied or partially unsatisfied) at the        或部分未履行）的交易價格的金額如下：
      end of the reporting period are as follows:

                                                                                                 2023            2022
                                                                                              RMB’000         RMB’000
                                                                                             人民幣千元           人民幣千元

      HONG KONG PROFITS TAX                                                        香港利得稅

      For the purpose of impairment testing, goodwill, brand name and       就 減 值 測 試 而 言，附 註 15 所 載 的 商 譽、具
      trademark and operating concession rights with indefinite useful      無限可使用年期的商號及商標和特許經營
      lives set out in note 15 have been allocated to twelve individual     權已分配至十二個個別的現金產生單位（「現
      cash-generating units (“CGUs”), comprising subsidiaries engaging      金產 生單 位」），該等單 位包括所從事 以下
      in the following businesses:                                          業務的附屬公司：

      •     provision of environmental engineering services;                •   提供環境工程服務；
      •     provision of sewage treatment services;                         •   提供污水處理服務；
      •     provision of hazardous waste treatment services;                •   提供危險廢品處理服務；
      •     provision of solid waste treatment services;                    •   提供固體廢物處理服務；
      •     manufacture and lease of aluminium formworks;                   •   製造及租賃鋁模架；
      •     provision of e-commerce services;                               •   提供電商服務；
      •     cultivation of flowers and plants;                              •   種植花朵及植物；
      •     manufacture and sale of plastic pipes and pipe fittings;        •   製造及銷售塑料管道及管件；
      •     manufacture and sale of waterproof materials;                   •   製造及銷售防水材料；

       The recoverable amount of each CGU has been determined               各現金產生單位之可收回金額乃基於現金
       based on value-in-use calculation using cash flow projections        流 量 預 測 計 算 之 使 用 價 值 釐 定，而 現 金 流
       which are based on financial budgets approved by management          量預測乃根據管理層審批之五年期財務預
       covering a five-year period. The growth rate used to extrapolate     算。超出五年期之現金流量使用 2% 之增長
       cash flows beyond the five-year period is 2% and the pre-tax         率 推 算，而 現 金 流 量 預 測 所 應 用 的 稅 前 貼
       discount rates applied to cash flow projections are as follows:      現率如下：

       Other key assumptions for the value in use calculation relate to     計算可使用價值的其他主要假設與包括預

       sales and gross margin. Such estimation is based on the units’       計 有 關。有 關 估 計 乃 基 於 單 位 的 過 往 表 現
       past performance and management‘s expectations for the               及 管 理 層 對 市 場 發 展 的 預 期 而 作 出。管 理
       market development. Management believes that any reasonably          層 認 為，任 何 該 等 假 設 可 能 出 現 的 任 何 合
       possible change in any of these assumptions would not cause the      理變動不會導致各現金產生單位各自的賬
       carrying amount of each CGU to exceed its recoverable amount.        面值超出其各自的可收回金額。

       concession rights of GZEP, which arose from the expected decline     因市場需求預期下降所致。
       in the market demand.

35. DEFERRED INCOME                                                          35. 遞延收益
      Deferred income represents government grants received by the             遞 延 收 益 指 本 集 團 收 取 的 政 府 補 助，作 為
      Group as financial subsidies for the renovation of factories and         其在廣東、長春、湖南、海南、大慶、湖北
      machineries or the construction of new factory premises in               及雲南等翻新工廠及機械或興建新工廠物
      Guangdong, Changchun, Hunan, Hainan, Daqing, Hubei, Yunnan,              業 的 財 政 補 貼，以 及 自 本 集 團 向 若 干 僱 員
      etc. and rental income received in advance from certain of the           預先收取的租賃員工宿舍租金收益。
      Group’s employees for leasing the staff quarters.

      The government grants are released to profit or loss over the            政府補助於有關房產預期可使用年限內按
      expected useful lives of the relevant premises by equal annual           年 以 等 額 撥 入 損 益 內 確 認，而 預 先 收 取 的
      instalments, while rental income received in advance is                  租金收益則按各自的租期以直線法確認。
      recognised on the straight-line basis over the respective lease
      terms.

37. RESERVES                                                                     37. 儲備
      The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements therein                     本集團本年度及過往年度的儲備金額及其
      for the current and prior years are presented in the consolidated                 變動已於綜合權益變動表內呈列。
      statement of changes in equity.

43. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                           43. 金融風險管理目標及政策
    AND POLICIES
      The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise borrowings                本集團的主要金融工具包括借款以及現金
      and cash and short-term deposits. The main purpose of these                    及 短 期 存 款。該 等 金 融 工 具 的 主 要 用 途 乃
      financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s operations.          為 本 集 團 的 營 運 籌 集 資 金。本 集 團 亦 有 其
      The Group has various other financial assets and liabilities such              他由業務直接產生的不同金融資產及負債，
      as trade and other receivables and trade and other payables,                   例如貿易應收款項及其他應收款項和貿易
      which arise directly from its operations.                                      應付款項及其他應付款項。

      The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are              本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為利率風
      interest rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk, liquidity risk and     險、外 幣 風 險、信 貸 風 險、流 動 資 金 風 險
      equity price risk. As the Group’s exposure to these risks is kept to a         及 股 本 價 格 風 險。由 於 本 集 團 所 承 擔 的 該
      minimum, the Group has not used any derivatives and other                      等 風 險 維 持 於 最 低 水 平，本 集 團 並 無 使 用
      instruments for hedging purposes. The Group does not hold or                   任何衍生工具及其他工具以作對沖之用。
      issue derivative financial instruments for trading purposes. The               本集團並無持有或發行衍生金融工具以作
      Board reviews and agrees policies for managing each of these                   買 賣 之 用。董 事 會 已 審 閱 並 同 意 該 等 風 險
      risks and they are summarised below.                                           管理的政策，茲概述如下。

      INTEREST RATE RISK                                                             利率風險
      In respect of the floating interest rate instruments, the Group is             浮動利率工具將導致本集團面臨現金流量
      subject to the cash flow interest rate risk, while for the fixed               利 率 風 險，而 固 定 利 率 工 具 將 導 致 本 集 團
      interest rate instruments, the Group is subject to fair value interest         面臨公允價值利率風險。
      rate risk.

      possible change in interest rate, with all other variables held                利 率 借 款 於 全 年 仍 未 償 還，且 在 所 有 其 他
      constant, of the Group’s profit before tax by assuming the floating            變 數 保 持 不 變 的 情 況 下，本 集 團 除 稅 前 溢
      rate borrowings outstanding at the end of the reporting period                 利對合理可能的利率變動的敏感度。
      were outstanding for the whole year.
